## Fishing and Recreational Harbours Regulations {#fishing-and-recreational-harbours-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/78-767
:::

[FISHING AND RECREATIONAL HARBOURS ACT](/eng/acts/F-24)

Registration 1978-10-06

Fishing and Recreational Harbours Regulations

P.C. 1978-3079 1978-10-04
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Fisheries and the Environment, pursuant to section 9 of
the [Fishing and Recreational Harbours Act](/eng/acts/F-24), is pleased
hereby to make the annexed Regulations respecting the administration and
development of certain fishing and recreational harbours in Canada.
:::

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2011-40,
s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 1"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-863974 .Part}

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Fishing and Recreational
    Harbours Act](/eng/acts/F-24); ([loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[berthage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[berthage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a charge for the occupation
    by a vessel of a berth or other space at a harbour; ([droit
    d'amarrage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[enforcement officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[enforcement officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
    designated as such pursuant to section 10 of the Act; ([agent
    d'exécution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[floating home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[floating home]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/98-387, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[harbour]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[harbour]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a scheduled harbour;
    ([port]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[harbour manager]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[harbour manager]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person appointed
    under section 27 of the Act or under any other Act of Parliament to
    manage a harbour; ([responsable du port]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[harbour property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[harbour property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any real property at
    a harbour, including marine facilities; ([propriété
    portuaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[lease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a lease referred to in section 8
    of the Act; ([bail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[length]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a commercial fishing vessel, the
      length shown in the fishing vessel registration of that vessel,
      and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a recreational or other vessel,
      the horizontal distance measured between perpendiculars erected at
      the extreme ends of the vessel; ([longueur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence referred to in
    section 8 of the Act; ([permis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vessel, the master, operator or
      person in charge of the vessel, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of goods, the agent, consignor,
      consignee or carrier of the goods;
      ([propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[wharfage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[wharfage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a charge for placing goods on
    a wharf or for loading goods on or unloading goods from a vessel at
    a wharf. ([droit de quayage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/83-598, s. 1
- SOR/98-387, s. 1
:::

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of the
definition [[scheduled harbour]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of
the Act, the harbours set out in Schedule I are fishing or recreational
harbours.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-863995 .Part}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations apply to
every scheduled harbour.

## [Leases and Licences]{.HTitleText1} {#h-863997 .Part}

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** No lease or licence shall
be for any term exceeding 20 years except with the approval of the
Governor in Council.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/83-598, s. 2
:::

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** No lease or licence of a
harbour or any part of a harbour shall be granted except on terms and
conditions that ensure access by the public to the harbour.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-498, s. 1
:::

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall erect, maintain or occupy a building, structure or other
  work at a scheduled harbour except under a lease or licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, except with the permission in
  writing of the harbour manager, install electric power lines, water
  supply lines or drainage pipes on harbour property.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-396, s. 1
:::

**[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall berth,
in a harbour, a vessel to be used as a residence unless there is an
authorization to berth the vessel in that harbour under a lease or
licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-387, s. 2
:::

## [General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-864012 .Part}

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall bring dangerous, explosive or combustible goods on
  harbour property unless the goods are required for the ordinary
  purposes of a vessel in a harbour and prior notice has been given to
  the harbour manager of the proposed location of the goods and of the
  manner in which they will be handled and dealt with.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the harbour manager has reasonable grounds to
  believe that the safety of the harbour or of the public may be
  endangered by the proposed location of any dangerous, explosive or
  combustible goods on harbour property or by the manner in which such
  goods are to be handled or dealt with on harbour property, he shall
  prohibit the goods from being brought on the harbour property or being
  so handled or dealt with, as the case may be.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall bring on harbour property goods the
  entry of which has been prohibited pursuant to subsection (2) or
  handle or deal with goods in a manner that has been prohibited
  pursuant to that subsection.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-498, s. 2
:::

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, in a
harbour,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} have an open fire on a vessel, or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} berth a vessel that, because of its size or dangerous
  condition, may endanger or damage the harbour facilities or other
  vessels;

without the approval of the harbour manager.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-498, s. 2
- SOR/83-598, s. 3
- SOR/98-387, s. 3(E)
:::

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** Where harbour property is
damaged in any manner by a vessel or vehicle, or by the use of machinery
or equipment, the damage shall be reported forthwith to the harbour
manager by the master or other person in charge of the vessel or the
operator of the vehicle, or the person who was in charge of the
machinery or equipment at the time the damage occurred.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-498, s. 3
:::

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** No person shall remove,
damage or destroy any notice posted at a harbour by the Minister.

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/80-498,
s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** No person shall obstruct
or impede in any way the docking or undocking of any vessel in a
harbour.

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** Where a harbour manager
has directed orally or by notice in writing the place where, the manner
in which and the time when a vessel is to be berthed, moored, moved,
loaded or unloaded, no person shall berth, moor, move, load or unload a
vessel except as so directed.

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/98-387,
s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, on
harbour property, without the approval of the harbour manager

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} engage in equipment or machinery tests that are
  likely to endanger harbour property or vessels or persons in the
  harbour; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} engage in repairs to the hull, machinery, tackle or
  gear of a vessel beyond normal routine maintenance.

## [Mooring Vessels]{.HTitleText1} {#h-864036 .Part}

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** No person shall permit
the lines of a vessel that is berthed or moored at a harbour to be made
fast to anything other than the facilities provided for berthing or
mooring or permit such lines to lie across any wharf or across any
channel in such a manner as to obstruct or interfere with any other
vessel, vehicle or person.

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** Where a vessel is moored
in a harbour, the master or person in charge shall, where necessary,
permit another vessel to move alongside and make fast to it.

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** Where two or more vessels
are lying at the same wharf, one vessel outside the other, and the
vessel on the outside does not have a gangway extending to the wharf,
the person in charge of the vessel lying nearer to the wharf shall allow
persons on the vessel farther from the wharf free and unencumbered
passage over its deck.

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a vessel is made fast to or secured alongside another vessel, one
  vessel outside the other,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the lines of the vessel that is so made fast or
    secured shall not, except in an emergency, be cut or cast off by any
    person unless that person gives notice of his intention to do so to
    the master or person in charge of that vessel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the master or person in charge of the inside
    vessel, when ready to leave harbour, may move any outside vessel if
    the owner or persons in charge of any such vessel cannot be located.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person moves a vessel pursuant to paragraph
  (1)(b), that person shall resecure any vessel that has been so moved.

<!-- -->

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  goods or articles fall overboard from a vessel, the master or person
  in charge of the vessel shall make every reasonable effort to recover
  those goods or articles.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the master or person in charge referred to in
  subsection (1) is unable to recover any goods or articles referred to
  therein, he shall report such failure to the harbour manager.

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** No person shall start a
fire on harbour property except at such time and in such place and
manner as is authorized by the harbour manager.

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** No person shall interfere
with fire-fighting equipment on a wharf or other harbour property or use
such equipment for any purpose except fighting fires.

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** No person shall supply
to, receive into or discharge from a vessel in a harbour or vehicle on
harbour property any gasoline or other fuel or oil except at such
location, in such manner and at such time as is authorized by the
harbour manager.

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** No person shall dispose
of garbage, sewage or wastes at a harbour except in a place especially
provided therefor or as directed by the harbour manager.

## [Berthage, Wharfage and Other Charges]{.HTitleText1} {#h-864052 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/87-99, s. 1

\]
:::

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** No berthage is payable in
respect of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any vessel, not engaged in trade, that is owned by
  Her Majesty in right of Canada or of any province or by the government
  of any country other than Canada; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any commercial fishing vessel of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} less than 13.5 m in length, at a harbour in the
    Provinces of Quebec, New Brunswick, Nova Scotia, Prince Edward
    Island and Newfoundland and Labrador, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any length at a harbour in the Provinces of
    Alberta, Saskatchewan and Manitoba, in the Yukon Territory or in the
    Northwest Territories.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-469, s. 1
- [SOR/2020-181, s. 6](#1258852-1259426){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1258852-1259426.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-26-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 26"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** No wharfage is payable in
respect of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any goods loaded on or unloaded from a vessel in
  respect of which berthage is not payable;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any motor vehicle embarking on or disembarking from a
  ferry; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any goods carried on a motor vehicle embarking on or
  disembarking from a ferry.

<!-- -->

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  charges for berthage of a Canadian commercial fishing vessel and for
  wharfage are set out in Schedules II and III respectively.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charges for storage on harbour property are set
  out in Schedule IV.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unused portions of prepaid berthage charges will be
  refunded after settlement at the daily rates.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-469, s. 2
- SOR/87-99, s. 2
- SOR/2002-381, s. 1
:::

**[[28.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.1 .sectionLabel}** Sections 26 to 28 do
not apply with respect to any harbour or portion thereof that, pursuant
to section 8 of the Act, is the subject of a lease to any person or an
agreement with the government of any province or any agency thereof for
the occupancy and use of that harbour or portion thereof where the
person, the government or the agency is designated as a harbour
authority in the lease or the agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/87-673, s. 1
:::

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** No person who is the
owner or operator of a vessel shall permit the vessel to leave a harbour
until all charges payable in respect of the vessel have been paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-469, s. 3
:::

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/81-469,
s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

## [Ticket Offences]{.HTitleText1} {#h-864079 .Part}

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Each
  offence described in those sections of the Regulations specified in
  column I of Schedule V is hereby designated an offence for the
  purposes of section 25 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the provisions of the [Criminal
  Code](/eng/acts/C-46), any enforcement officer who believes on
  reasonable and probable grounds that a person has committed an offence
  referred to in subsection (1) may lay an information and issue and
  serve a summons by completing a ticket in the form prescribed by
  subsection (5), affixing his signature thereto and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} delivering the summons to the person at the time
    the alleged offence is alleged to have been committed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} mailing the summons to the latest known address of
    the person.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person on whom a summons has been served as
  described in subsection (2) may, at any time prior to the expiration
  of the time specified in the summons, voluntarily enter a plea of
  guilty by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} completing the "Plea of Guilty" portion of the
    summons and affixing his signature thereto; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivering or mailing the summons, together with a
    certified cheque or money order in the amount of the fine specified
    in the summons, to the court indicated therein.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The fine for an offence described in a section of the
  Regulations specified in Column I of an item of Schedule V is the
  amount set out opposite that section in Column II, III or IV,
  whichever is appropriate.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The forms of tickets set out in Parts I to X of
  Schedule VI in respect of the provinces specified in those Parts are
  hereby prescribed as the forms referred to in subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/81-229, s. 1
:::
